Cytosol: Unlikely — cytosolic proteins lack signal peptides.<br>  - B. Nucleus: No nuclear localization signal (NLS) pattern (e.g., SV40-like PKKKRKV) is present.<br>  - C. Additionally, the downstream portion of the protein (AARPLPASAAAELVLQDGATGNGDEVSELMGAAEEEAAGLCEEGNEECVERRMLRDAHLDYIYTQKRNRP) is highly hydrophilic, rich in charged residues (D, E, R, K), and lacks any second transmembrane helix that would be expected for a genuine membrane anchor. The hydrophilic mature protein after the cleavage site is consistent with a secreted protein.<br><br>The presence of the cleavable signal peptide with a functional A-X-A cleavage site, combined with the hydrophilic nature of the mature protein, strongly indicates secretion to the extracellular region.<br><br>2. answer: C</td>
